def test_requests_under_limit_succeed(client):

    

    for i in range(5):

        response = client.post(
            "/shorten",
            json={
                "long_url": f"https://example{i}.com"
            }
        )

        assert response.status_code == 201



def test_rate_limit_exceeded(client):

    for i in range(5):

        response = client.post(
            "/shorten",
            json={
                "long_url": f"https://example{i}.com"
            }
        )

        assert response.status_code == 201

    response = client.post(
        "/shorten",
        json={
            "long_url": "https://blocked.com"
        }
    )

    assert response.status_code == 429

    assert response.json()["detail"] == (
        "Rate limit exceeded. Please try again later."
    )


def test_rate_limit_counts_multiple_requests(client):

    success_count = 0

    for i in range(10):

        response = client.post(
            "/shorten",
            json={
                "long_url": f"https://example{i}.com"
            }
        )

        if response.status_code == 201:
            success_count += 1

    assert success_count == 5


def test_rate_limit_refills(client):

    for i in range(5):
        client.post(
            "/shorten",
            json={
                "long_url": f"https://example{i}.com"
            }
        )

    blocked = client.post(
        "/shorten",
        json={
            "long_url": "https://blocked.com"
        }
    )

    assert blocked.status_code == 429

    time.sleep(1.1)

    response = client.post(
        "/shorten",
        json={
            "long_url": "https://allowed-after-refill.com"
        }
    )

    assert response.status_code == 201
==> ./backend/tests/integration/test_redirect.py <==
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone


def create_url(client, long_url="https://google.com"):
    response = client.post(
        "/shorten",
        json={
            "long_url": long_url
        }
    )

    return response.json()["short_code"]


def test_redirect_existing_url(client):
    code = create_url(client)

    response = client.get(
        f"/{code}",
        follow_redirects=False
    )

    assert response.status_code == 302
    assert response.headers["location"] == "https://google.com/"


def test_redirect_different_url(client):
    code = create_url(
        client,
        "https://github.com"
    )

    response = client.get(
        f"/{code}",
        follow_redirects=False
    )

    assert response.status_code == 302
    assert response.headers["location"] == "https://github.com/"


def test_redirect_unknown_short_code(client):
    response = client.get(
        "/abc123",
        follow_redirects=False
    )

    assert response.status_code == 404
    assert response.json()["detail"] == "URL not found"


def test_redirect_invalid_short_code_format(client):
    response = client.get(
        "/$$$",
        follow_redirects=False
    )

    assert response.status_code == 400
==> ./backend/tests/integration/test_urls.py <==
def register_user(client):
    client.post(
        "/auth/register",
        json={
            "username": "john",
            "email": "john@example.com",
            "password": "secret123"
        }
    )


def login_user(client):
    response = client.post(
        "/auth/login",
        json={
            "username": "john",
            "password": "secret123"
        }
    )

    return response.json()["access_token"]


def auth_headers(client):
    token = login_user(client)

    return {
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"
    }

def test_create_short_url_anonymous(client):
    response = client.post(
        "/shorten",
        json={
            "long_url" : "https://www.google.com"
        }
    )

    assert response.status_code == 201

    body = response.json()

    assert body["message"] == "Success"
    assert "short_code" in body
    assert "short_url" in body


def test_create_short_url_authenticated_user(client):
    register_user(client)

    response = client.post(
        "/shorten",
        json={
            "long_url" : "https://www.github.com"
        },
        headers=auth_headers(client)
    )

    assert response.status_code == 201

    body = response.json()

    assert "short_code" in body
    assert "short_url" in body

def test_create_multiple_urls_returns_different_codes(client):
    response1 = client.post(
        "/shorten",
        json={
            "long_url" : "https://www.google.com"
        }
    )

    response2 = client.post(
            "/shorten",
            json={
                "long_url" : "https://www.google.com"
            }
        )

    code1 = response1.json()["short_code"]
    code2 = response2.json()["short_code"]

    assert code1 != code2


def test_create_short_url_invalid_url(client):
    response = client.post(
        "/shorten",
        json={
            "long_url": "not-a-valid-url"
        }
    )

    assert response.status_code == 422


def test_get_my_urls_requires_authentication(client):
    response = client.get("/api/my-urls")

    assert response.status_code == 401


def test_get_my_urls_returns_user_urls(client):
    register_user(client)

    headers = auth_headers(client)

    client.post(
        "/shorten",
        json={
            "long_url": "https://google.com"
        },
        headers=headers
    )

    client.post(
        "/shorten",
        json={
            "long_url": "https://github.com"
        },
        headers=headers
    )

    response = client.get(
        "/api/my-urls",
        headers=headers
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    urls = response.json()

    assert len(urls) == 2

    assert urls[0]["short_code"]
    assert urls[0]["short_url"]
    assert urls[0]["long_url"]

==> ./backend/tests/unit/test_base62.py <==
from app.utils.base62 import decode, encode

def test_encode_decode_roundtrip():

    original_id = 12345

    short_code = encode(original_id)

    decoded_id = decode(short_code)

    assert decoded_id == original_id

def test_encode_decode_roundtrip_multiple_ids():

    ids = [
            1,
            10,
            100,
            999,
            12345,
            9999999
    ]

    for original_id in ids:

        shortcode = encode(original_id)

        decoded_id = decode(shortcode)

        assert decoded_id == original_id


def test_encode_returns_string():

    short_code = encode(1)

    assert isinstance(
        short_code,
        str
    )

    assert len(short_code) > 0
==> ./backend/tests/unit/test_jwt.py <==
from app.utils.jwt import create_access_token, decode_access_token


def test_create_and_decode_token():
    user_id = 123
    token = create_access_token(user_id)
    payload = decode_access_token(token)

    assert payload["sub"] == str(user_id)

def test_token_is_string():
    user_id = 123
    token = create_access_token(user_id)

    assert isinstance(token,str)

    assert len(token)>0


def test_different_users_get_different_tokens():
    
    token1 = create_access_token(1)
    token2 = create_access_token(2)

    assert token1 != token2




==> ./backend/tests/unit/test_password.py <==
from app.utils.password import hash_password, verify_password


def test_hash_password():
    password = "secret123"

    hashed = hash_password(password)

    assert hashed != password


def test_verify_password_success():
    password = "secret123"

    hashed = hash_password(password)

    assert verify_password(password,hashed)

def test_verify_password_failure():
    password = "secret123"

    hashed = hash_password(password)

    assert not verify_password("wrongpassword",hashed)
